Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the lists space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 204 and the convergence rate is O(50/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to lists theory, we decompose the problem into 204 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 50. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.
